# Skills · P · part 273

912 entries, pymatgen to pyrsm-visualize. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pymatgen**: Use when a user asks to parse material formulas, inspect compositions, parse or summarize crystal structures, convert CIF/POSCAR/JSON/CSSR/XSF structures, or analyze symmetry through the deployed chemistry pymatgen gateway service. Triggers on pymatgen, materials, composition, formula parsing, CIF, POSCAR, crystal structure, lattice, space group, symmetry, primitive cell, or structure conversion. ([Bin-Cao/BaiZe](https://github.com/Bin-Cao/BaiZe/tree/HEAD/.baize/skills/pymatgen) · ★2)
- **pymatgen**: Computational materials science in Python with pymatgen (&gt;= 2026.9) and mp-api: periodic Structure & Molecule validation, CIF/POSCAR/VASP/Q-Chem I/O, tolerance-swept space-group symmetry, supercell/substitution transformations, local thermodynamic phase diagrams, and bounded Materials Project queries. ([ualberta-rcg/drac-agent-skills](https://github.com/ualberta-rcg/drac-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/community-skills/skills/pymatgen) · ★2)
